# MyNclex Sessions — 2026-05

Archived session log for 2026-05. Newest first.

## Session — 2026-05-26 (Library 11.1a applied + Slice 11.2a + 11.2b + dev-server warnings cleared)

Single session, three coherent threads on the new
`claude/flamboyant-pike-b40055` worktree. Started by closing the
2026-05-25 carry-over (11.1a schema applied to dev — and patched in
the process), then shipped both halves of Slice 11.2 end-to-end.

### Thread 1 — Slice 11.1a schema, patched + applied to dev (commit `b73215d`)

The migration committed last session (`23c23e7`) couldn't actually
run. Two hard blockers surfaced against the real
`nclex_bank_items` shape before `apply_migration` was called:

1. **FK type mismatch.** Planned
   `nclex_library_embed_answers.item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES
   nclex_bank_items(item_id)`, but `nclex_bank_items.item_id` is
   **TEXT** (the bank uses text PKs everywhere — `parent_case_id`,
   `trend_id` confirm the pattern). FK rejected at CREATE TABLE.
2. **Missing tutor ownership.** `nclex_bank_items` has **no
   `tutor_id` column** — the bank is QAcademy-owned, not per-tutor.
   The planned `nclex_library_embed_answers_tutor_select` policy
   referenced `i.tutor_id = auth.uid()`; would fail at CREATE
   POLICY.

Sam picked **Option C** of three options I surfaced (A: patch in
place, B: rework as polymorphic now, C: slice it out) — drop
`nclex_library_embed_answers` from this migration entirely.
Sections 11 + 24 of the migration file became comment-only
placeholders preserving the planning-doc numbering; embed-answers
re-lands as a polymorphic BANK / TUTOR target table when slice
11.15 ships the embedded-question consumption path (memory
`project_library_embed_answers_deferred` captures the design
constraint).

Applied the patched migration to `mynclex-dev`. Smoke-test
counts: 8 tables, 1 domain, 4 functions, 3 triggers, 33 RLS
policies. Memory cleanup — deleted the
`project_library_11_1a_migration_unapplied` warning file; added
`project_library_embed_answers_deferred`; updated build-phase
memory + BUILD_LIST + tutor-library planning doc header to
reflect the 8-table reality.

### Thread 2 — Dev-server startup warnings cleared (commit `2c66d46`)

Two cosmetic warnings on `npm run dev` were noise; explained both
to Sam (multiple lockfiles + Turbopack workspace-root inference;
`middleware` → `proxy` rename in Next.js 16) before touching code.

- **Lockfile / workspace root.** A stray empty 84-byte
  `package-lock.json` was sitting at `C:\Users\confi\` (no
  matching `package.json` — pure accidental `npm install` cruft).
  Next.js was walking up to it and picking the wrong root. Deleted
  the stray. Added a `findProjectRoot(__dirname)` walker in
  `next.config.ts` that climbs to the nearest ancestor with
  `node_modules` and pins `turbopack.root` to it. Important
  because session worktrees under `.claude/worktrees/` share the
  parent repo's `node_modules` — the naive `turbopack.root =
  __dirname` crashed Turbopack module resolution for `next`
  itself (which is how we learned the real shape).
- **`middleware` → `proxy`.** Renamed `middleware.ts` → `proxy.ts`
  via `git mv` (history preserved); renamed the exported function
  `middleware()` → `proxy()`. Three other places mention the term
  (`lib/supabase/server.ts` comment, `lib/access/README.md`,
  `CLAUDE.md` rule #6) — all three updated to say `proxy.ts`.
  Next.js docs cite a codemod for this; the rename is two edits so
  did it by hand.

### Thread 3 — Slice 11.2a: folder CRUD (commit `a2e7e7b`)

First slice that talks to the real schema. Scoped end-to-end up
front so the build was mechanical:

  Read it carefully, pulled four moves into 11.2a: the

  folders not gating visibility), the `AllFolders` grid layout

  Folders lens "All folders" entry, and the per-folder card meta

  note in this folder"* — depends on the editor route landing in
  11.2b.
- **Files new (7) in `lib/library/`:** `types.ts`, `queries.ts`,
  `actions.ts`, `format.ts` *(slot held for 11.2b)*, `folder-rows.tsx`,

- **Files modified:** `lib/library/home-shell.tsx` (flattened from
  `home/`, made data-aware — accepts folders + selected props,
  wires `+ New folder`, branches main pane on URL state),
  `app/(app)/tutor/library/page.tsx` (server component reading

- **URL state.** `?folder=<uuid>` for a specific folder,

### Thread 4 — Slice 11.2b: note CRUD + dedicated editor route (commit pending)

Closes the 11.2 sub-arc. Sam locked the design picks up front via a
3-question `AskUserQuestion` round before any code (dedicated
route ✓, split into 11.2a/b ✓, flatten home subfolder ✓), then
the build was driven by both the CD prototype's `note-editor.jsx`
+ the older in-repo `tutor-library-mockup.html`. The two
prototypes disagreed in places — reconciled with a table of picks
(see commit message for the matrix). Notable picks: kept the
global tutor sidebar (older mockup wins over CD's hide-the-nav),
adopted CD's inline meta row over older-mockup's right-rail
chip placement, made pillars multi-select (gap-review-settled),
38px → 34px serif title (middle ground).

- **Modal-first new-note flow** — title (2..120 chars) + folder
  dropdown (defaults to currently-selected folder) + NCLEX-pillar
  multi-select (≥1 required, popover with short-form chip labels).
  Submit → `createNoteAction` → routes to
  `/tutor/library/note/<id>`.
- **Editor route at `/tutor/library/note/[note_id]`.** Server
  component fetches the note + folders in parallel; RLS-gated, 404
  on miss / not-yours.
- **Editor three-zone layout.** Sticky toolbar (breadcrumb left,
  save badge / Draft pill / disabled Publish / Save right) above
  `1fr 240px` grid (main + right rail). Rail hides at ≤1080px.
- **Inline meta row** between text headers and body — Folder chip
  (popover reparent) · Pillars chips + `+ Add` (popover) · Tags
  chip + free-text input. Three new atom files —
  `pillar-picker.tsx`, `folder-picker.tsx`, `tag-input.tsx` —
  reused between the new-note modal and the editor.
- **Body as a textarea.** Schema stores JSONB block array; 11.2b
  reads/writes a single paragraph block via
  `bodyToText` / `textToBody` helpers. The same shape Tiptap will
  produce in 11.5 — persisted JSON survives the upgrade.
  Embedded-question / drug-card / table / image blocks are
  preserved-not-edited if they exist (they don't yet — every 11.2b
  note has the single-paragraph shape).
- **Right rail — 5 sections.**
  - **Status** — Draft pill + visibility-set-on-publish muted line
    + last-save with a 30s-interval relative-time formatter.
  - **Outline** — best-effort heading detection from textarea text
    (`# `, `## `, ALL-CAPS short lines). Real outline lands with
    11.5.
  - **Embedded questions** — count via body-walk for
    `embedded_questions` blocks. Always 0 in 11.2b; auto-lights up
    when 11.15 ships the picker.
  - **Used in** — count via PostgREST embed
    `nclex_tutor_library_note_attachments(count)`. Always 0 in
    11.2b; auto-lights up when 11.11 ships note-as-activity
    attachment.
  - **Guards** — live explainer of the current save model.
- **Breadcrumb in the toolbar** (added as Sam's final polish call
  after the rest was tested green). Replaces the `← Back` button
  with `Library / <folder name> / <note title>` — first two
  clickable, last bold and non-clickable. Goes through a unified
  `attemptLeave(href)` guard so dirty + crumb-click fires
  `DiscardConfirm`. For root notes the middle segment is skipped
  (Sam's pick — Option A in the discussion). The folder list and
  All-folders crumbs got the `Library` link too for consistency.
- **Save model.** Explicit Save (no debounced autosave yet — that
  ships with Tiptap in 11.5). `version_id` rotates on every UPDATE
  (forward-compat scaffolding for the two-tabs guard in 11.5).
  `beforeunload` browser warning when dirty.
- **Validation** mirrors at three layers: client modal (inline red
  copy), server action (auth check + length caps + pillar
  membership + tag length + dup-check), DB (CHECK constraint on
  `nclex_pillar` domain + ≥1 pillars + RLS).
- **Files new (8):** `lib/library/format.ts`,
  `pillar-picker.tsx`, `folder-picker.tsx`, `tag-input.tsx`,
  `new-note-modal.tsx`, `notes-list.tsx`, `note-editor.tsx`,
  `app/(app)/tutor/library/note/[note_id]/page.tsx`.
- **Files modified (7):** `lib/library/queries.ts` (note-list +
  note-with-attachment-count reads), `lib/library/actions.ts`
  (`createNoteAction` + `updateNoteAction` + pillar/tag validators),
  `lib/library/types.ts` (`NclexPillar`, `LibraryNote`,
  `LibraryNoteListRow`, `LibraryNoteForEdit`, form-value types),
  `lib/library/home-shell.tsx` (wire `+ New note`, render
  `<NotesList>` for selected folders, mount `<NewNoteModal>`),
  `lib/library/all-folders-grid.tsx` + `notes-list.tsx` (clickable
  `Library` crumb), `app/(app)/tutor/library/page.tsx` (fetch
  notes for the selected folder), `styles/library.css` (large
  block — notes list rows, meta chips, popovers, tag chips,

### Sam's testing — five rounds, all green

The slice was QA'd in five passes: (1) folder modal validation +
sidebar wiring, (2) note creation flow + editor first-load, (3)
edit + save round-trips + dirty guards, (4) the breadcrumb
behaviours after the polish add, (5) the two new rail sections
(Embedded questions + Used in) showing the right zero-state copy.
Each round was rebuilt from a fresh tcsc + dev-server-log check
before being handed off. No bugs found in any pass.

## Session — 2026-05-24 (Slice 8 UI rebuild — CD-driven, 3 PRs)

Day-after follow-on to the Slice 8 a/b/c ship. The previous session's
tutor surface (an inbox split view built from a written spec) was
visually weak — Sam pushed back: *"the interphase is not nice at
all"*. Brief went to Claude Design, which returned a thorough
prototype bundle: README + 3 hi-fi variants per audience (tutor V1
polished inbox / V2 today-action-plan / V3 simple; admin V1 ops
dashboard / V2 programme funnel / V3 simple) + shared shell/data/
tokens. Picked Tutor V1 + Admin V1. Shipped via 3 serial commits on
the same session branch.

This was the first real test of the **CD-prototype-then-implement
workflow** (now captured as
[`feedback_cd_prototype_then_implement.md`](../../memory/feedback_cd_prototype_then_implement.md)
in user memory). Sam's verdict: *"this was really good — letting CD
do a prototype. The designs look really nice and like a real
dashboard kind of thing."*

### PR 1 — data layer (commit `06c83a3`)

Pure foundations; no UI consumers yet. Three new files in `lib/enquiries/`:

- **`format.ts`** gains `urgencyTier(iso, status)` + `URGENCY_META`
  (hot / warm / aging / cold / done — same thresholds as the CD
  prototype). Drives the tutor list's left aging strip + the admin
  SLA badges + the KPI "Needs reply now" count.
- **`aggregations.ts`** — pure functions over the row list, no DB
  hops:
  - `computeTutorStats(enquiries, now?)` → KPI block for the tutor
    inbox: `hotCount`, `open {new, contacted, total}`, `avgResponseSec`
    + 30-day-vs-prev-30d delta, `convertedThisMonth`,
    `conversionRate30d`, `topChannel`, `channelMix`.
  - `computeAdminStats(rows, now?)` → KPI block + tutor scoreboard
    + channel mix for the admin dashboard. Includes 4 sparkline
    series (`weeklySpark` / `hotWaitingSpark` / `avgResponseSpark` /
    `conversionSpark`) — 8 trailing weeks each, computed via shared
    `bucketCounts` + `bucketSampled` helpers (the former counts rows
    matching a predicate, the latter accumulates sum/count pairs
    for ratios/means).
  - Tutor scoreboard rows sort by open work, then total volume,
    then name — so "who needs my attention now" surfaces first.
- **`templates.ts`** — four quick-reply templates with {name} /
  {tutor} / {programme} placeholders + `renderTemplate(template,
  ctx)` helper:
  - `cohort-dates` — "Send cohort dates + fees"
  - `payment-plan` — "Share payment plan"
  - `schedule-call` — "Schedule a call"
  - `custom` — opens an empty composer

### PR 2 — tutor V1 polished inbox (commits `8d40dc0` + `e7c3b05`)

`/tutor/programme/<id>/enquiries` rebuilt as the CD V1 design. Layout
stacks: **KPI strip (5 cards)** → **toolbar (filter chips + search)**
→ **split (day-grouped list left, detail right)**.

- **KPI strip** — Needs reply now / Open (with new vs in-progress
  sub-line) / Avg response time (with up/down delta) / Converted this
  month (with conversion rate) / Top channel (with icon swatch). The
  Needs-reply-now card tints red when count > 0. Strip is fed by
  `computeTutorStats(enquiries)` on the server.
- **Toolbar** — filter chips with counts (Open default) + a search
  input that filters name / email / message text live.
- **List (left column)** — day-grouped (Today / Yesterday / This
  week / Older) with sticky group headers. Each row: 3px urgency
  strip on the far left (coloured by tier) → name + relative time +
  2-line snippet + status pill + channel chips with WhatsApp
  highlighted green → red hot-dot on the right edge for hot leads.
  Active row gets a light blue background + the name turns navy.
- **Detail (right column)** —
  - **Head**: 44px gradient avatar (initials) + name + status pill
    + clickable email/phone + "Received Xh ago · Contacted Yh ago"
    + urgency badge + "Prefers:" channel pills.
  - **Reply bar** (the killer feature): WhatsApp / Call / Email
    CTAs. Each click opens the channel deep-link (`wa.me/<num>?text=…`
    / `tel:…` / `mailto:…`) AND fires `markContactedAction` in the
    same click. "Mark contacted only" ghost button covers the
    off-platform case (tutor reached out via Whatsapp on their phone,
    didn't go through the platform). The auto-mark step silently
    no-ops if the lead is already CONTACTED.
  - **Body**: Message block (teal accent stripe, falls back to
    italic empty-state copy when null). Quick replies grid (2×2 of
    template buttons; chooseChannel picks the lead's preferred channel
    from their `preferred_contact[]`, falls back to WhatsApp if
    available else Email). Notes (yellow-tinted card, 800ms debounce
    auto-save, 3-second green "Saved · just now" flash).
  - **Footer**: enquiry ID (last 4 chars uppercase mono) + "Close
    lead" ghost button.
- **React 19 strict-mode polish** caught by lint:
  - Derived `selectedId` via useMemo from a `selectedIdRaw` user
    override + the active filter — no `setState` inside a `useEffect`
    (the cascading-renders rule).
  - `NotesEditor` uses `key={enquiry.enquiry_id}` for clean
    state-reset on switch rather than a reset effect.
  - Binary `recentlySaved` flag replaces tracking `Date.now()` in
    render (the impure-function-during-render rule).
  - Donut SVG in PR 3 uses a `reduce` pre-compute pass (no mid-render
    mutation of `offset`).
- **New `.ti-*` style family** covers everything (KPI strip cards,
  filter chips, search input, split layout, list rows, detail head,
  reply bar with the WhatsApp `#25D366` green, body sections,
  templates grid, notes, footer). Old `.enq-*` tutor classes (the
  earlier card-stack inbox) replaced; admin board still on `.enq-*`
  until PR 3 lands `.ao-*`.
- **Width fix** (separate commit `e7c3b05`): page inherited
  `.pp-page { max-width: 720px }` from the payment-plans page, which
  was crushing the dashboard. Added a new `.ti-page` wrapper (1480px
  cap so very wide screens don't stretch the detail pane to
  unreadable line lengths). Other tutor pages keep `.pp-page` — the
  payment-plans form genuinely wants 720px.

### PR 3 — admin V1 operations dashboard (commit `1364b92`)

`/admin/enquiries` rebuilt as the CD V1 ops dashboard. Layout stacks:
**KPI strip (4 cards w/ sparklines)** → **insights row (scoreboard +
donut)** → **filterable table**.

- **KPI strip** — Volume this week (with weekly sparkline) / Hot
  leads waiting (red when > 0; sparkline) / Avg first response
  (faster-is-better delta) / Conversion rate (percentage-points
  delta). The Volume card carries a red "X BREACHES" corner flag
  when there are NEW leads ≥24h old (the SLA threshold).
- **Tutor SLA scoreboard** (left panel of insights row) — one row
  per tutor: avatar + name / volume / open count (red bold when > 3
  open) / avg reply time with a tier-coloured bar
  (green <4h / amber <12h / red ≥12h) / conversion rate
  (green > 50% / red < 20%). Sorted by open work → volume → name so
  "who needs my attention now" surfaces first.
- **Channel mix donut** (right panel) — 110px SVG with the four
  channel slices (WhatsApp green / Call blue / SMS purple / Email
  grey), centre shows the top channel + its percentage, legend rows
  show all four with their percentages. Insight callout below
  ("WhatsApp leads the channel mix… replies under 4 hours convert
  noticeably more often").
- **Filterable table** — Lead (avatar + name + email) / Programme ·
  Tutor / Channels / Age / Status pill / SLA badge ("On track" /
  "Xh waiting" / "SLA breach · Xh" / "—" / "In progress") /
  "Open ↗" link. Toolbar above: status select (with counts per
  status), programme select (only when ≥2 programmes have
  enquiries), tutor select (only when ≥2 tutors have leads). All
  filtering is client-side over the same fetched rows.
- Status changes intentionally NOT surfaced on the admin
  surface — each row's "Open ↗" hops to that programme's tutor
  enquiries page where the actions already live (SUPER_ADMIN
  bypass on the tutor RLS lets the admin land there).
- Inline `Sparkline` + `Donut` SVG components (no library
  dependency). Donut uses a reduce-based pre-compute pass —
  React 19 strict mode forbids mid-render mutation of the
  rolling `offset` variable.
- New `.ao-*` style family for the dashboard. Old `.enq-admin-*`
  family from Slice 8c left in place for one slice before deletion.
- Page wrapper `.ao-page` (1480px max + 20px padding) — same
  treatment as the tutor inbox so the dashboard uses the full
  available viewport.

### Scope note added to `lib/enquiries/types.ts`

Sam raised an architectural forward-looking question: today's
`lib/enquiries/` covers only programme enquiries, but the name is
generic. What happens when a second enquiry type appears (e.g.
general support, institutional sales)? Outcome: the module gets a
load-bearing header comment with the split plan — refactor to
`lib/enquiries/{shared,programme,<new>}/` when the time comes;
shared status enum + format helpers go up; DB tables stay
correctly prefixed and don't move; ~1-2 hour mechanical refactor.
**Don't pre-split** — wait for the second concrete type so the
right shared/per-type cut becomes visible.

### Other artefacts

- 8 seeded enquiries (Ghanaian nurse personas — Ama Boateng, Kojo
  Asante, Esi Owusu, Yaw Mensah, Adwoa Nkrumah, Akosua Frimpong,
  Kwesi Tetteh, Abena Sarpong) across both dev programmes, with

plus the PR 2 + PR 3 panel rewrites land on top of the existing
files (no new path).

shells.

## Session — 2026-05-23 (Payments Slice 8 — programme enquiries, all three sub-slices)

Same-day follow-on session to 7e. Closed the off-platform / contact-first
gap: programmes that don't expose an on-page commitment path now have a
working Contact form, a tutor surface to triage the leads, and an admin
view across all programmes. Three commits on the session branch:
`1fd2a53` (8a), `e0b8bf6` (8b), `319d8f6` (8c).

### 8a — schema + public submission (commit `1fd2a53`)

- **Migration** `20260613120000_slice_8a_programme_enquiries.sql` applied
  to mynclex-dev:
  - `nclex_programme_enquiries` table: `enquiry_id` (UUID PK),
    `programme_id` (FK CASCADE), `name`, `email`, `phone?`,
    `preferred_contact TEXT[]` (default `['EMAIL']`, subset of
    CALL/SMS/WHATSAPP/EMAIL), `message?`, `status` (default 'NEW',
    CHECK in NEW/FORWARDED/CONVERTED/CLOSED — FORWARDED later renamed
    in 8b), `forwarded_at?`, `converted_enrolment_id?` (FK SET NULL),
    `admin_notes?`, timestamps.
  - Two CHECK constraints lifted from the waitlist exactly: at least
    one preferred channel, phone-required when any phone channel is
    chosen.
  - Three indexes: UNIQUE partial on `(programme_id, lower(email))
    WHERE status IN ('NEW','FORWARDED')` for de-dup; full
    `(programme_id, status)` for the tutor queue; partial
    `(lower(email))` for 8c's matcher.
  - RLS: tutor SELECT scoped to own programmes; SUPER_ADMIN ALL bypass.
    **No raw INSERT/UPDATE policies** — public writes only via the RPC
    below; tutor/admin writes via service-role gating ownership in TS.
  - `nclex_submit_enquiry(p_programme_id, p_name, p_email, p_phone,
    p_preferred_contact, p_message)` RPC, SECURITY DEFINER, granted to
    anon + authenticated. Validates: non-empty name, valid email
    regex, preferred-contact subset of allowed, phone present when any
    phone channel is chosen, programme is enquiry-eligible (PUBLISHED
    AND tutor active AND (off-platform OR price-hidden)). Idempotent
    on (programme, email): a repeat while still open returns the
    existing id — no email-enumeration signal. Smoke-tested all four
    branches on dev (eligible → INSERT; duplicate → same id; ineligible
    → "not accepting"; WhatsApp w/o phone → "phone required").
- **Code:**
  - Extracted `CONTACT_OPTIONS` + `preferredContactNeedsPhone()` into
    `lib/discovery/contact-options.ts`. Waitlist switched to import
    from there — prevents drift if a channel is added.
  - New `lib/discovery/enquiry-actions.ts` — `submitEnquiryAction`
    forwards to the RPC after light client-side validation. Maps the
    "not accepting enquiries" error to a friendly "no longer
    accepting" message (the only branch a normal user can realistically
    hit by accident — e.g., the tutor switched modes mid-session).
  - New `app/(public)/programmes/[id]/enquiry-cta.tsx` — modal mirrors
    the waitlist's structure (same chrome, contact-method group,
    conditional-phone, success screen). Takes a `reason` prop
    (`'PRICE_HIDDEN' | 'OFF_PLATFORM'`) so the blurb fits the reason.
  - Detail page CTA branching reshaped: introduced `canEnrol /
    canWaitlist / canEnquire` as mutually exclusive states. `canEnquire
    = !canEnrol && !canWaitlist && (off-platform || price-hidden)` —
    catches everything the other two miss, including the previously
    dead-ended **tutor-led off-platform with no joinable cohort** case.
    The disabled "coming soon" stub now only fires when the programme
    is truly stuck (e.g. on-platform with no headline price).

### 8b — tutor enquiry queue + status rename (commit `e0b8bf6`)

- **Nav:** `Enquiries` entry added to `TUTOR_PROGRAMME_NAV` before
  Students (funnel order: enquiry → enrolment → student). Icon `mail`.
- **`lib/enquiries/` feature folder** mirroring `lib/enrolments/`:
  - `types.ts` — `EnquiryStatus`, `Enquiry`. Uses `ContactChannel`
    from the shared contact-options module.
  - `queries.ts` — `getEnquiriesForProgramme(programmeId)`. Authed
    client is enough; tutor SELECT RLS scopes correctly.
  - `actions.ts` — three actions (`markContactedAction`,
    `markClosedAction`, `saveAdminNotesAction`). Pattern: gate
    ownership with authed client → write under service role. Matches
    `lib/enrolments/actions.ts` and the waitlist convert/dismiss
    pattern, because `nclex_programme_enquiries` has no tutor UPDATE
    policy (only SUPER_ADMIN ALL).
  - `format.ts` — `statusPill()`, `channelLabel()`,
    `formatEnquiryDateTime()`.
- **Route** `/tutor/programme/<id>/enquiries` — page.tsx + a client
  `EnquiriesPanel`. First attempt was a card stack (`enq-card`); after
  Sam said "the interphase is not nice at all", rebuilt as an inbox
  split view (filter chips above; scannable list on the left with
  status-dot · name · time · channel initials + 1-line snippet; full
  detail on the right with structured meta block, message, notes,
  actions). Notes textarea moved to the detail pane only — was always
  visible per row in the first iteration, which was noisy. Selection
  state is automatic: first row of active filter is selected; if the
  pick falls out of view when the filter changes, jumps to the new
  first row. Sam flagged the visual still wasn't right; CD prototype
  will drive the next pass. Data model + actions don't need to change
  for that.
- **Style** `styles/enquiries.css` covers both the split view and the
  status/channel pill family. Mobile (≤720px) stacks the two columns.
- **Status rename `FORWARDED → CONTACTED`** (migration
  `20260614120000_slice_8b_rename_forwarded_to_contacted.sql`): Sam
  surfaced that "forwarded" was platform-perspective (came from the
  design-handoff phrasing where QAcademy was meant to forward the
  enquiry via email) but v1 has no auto-forward — the button actually
  records the tutor's act of contacting the student. Renamed end-to-end:
  loosen CHECK → UPDATE existing FORWARDED rows to CONTACTED → re-tighten
  CHECK with new value → rename column `forwarded_at → contacted_at` →
  rebuild both partial indexes that referenced the old value. TS
  propagation across types, format helper, actions, queries, panel
  filter key + chip label + button label.

### 8c — auto-convert trigger + admin queue (commit `319d8f6`)

- **Migration** `20260615120000_slice_8c_auto_convert_enquiry.sql`:
  `AFTER INSERT` trigger on `nclex_enrolments`, SECURITY DEFINER
  (needs to read `nclex_users.email` past self-read RLS and write to
  `nclex_programme_enquiries` past tutor-scoped policies — safe
  because the trigger only fires from a legitimate enrolment INSERT
  and operates strictly on `programme_id + lower(email)` matches).
  Looks up the enrolled student's email, then UPDATEs any open
  enquiry (NEW or CONTACTED) for the same programme to CONVERTED with
  `converted_enrolment_id = NEW.enrolment_id`. Fires for every
  enrolment path — tutor-add, on-platform checkout, future — no
  "we forgot to call the matcher" risk.
- **Verified on dev** with a synthetic enquiry (seeded against an
  existing test-user's email) + INSERT into nclex_enrolments with
  `enrolment_source = 'SELF_PAID'` (TUTOR_ADDED requires
  `enrolled_by_user_id` per the CHECK `nclex_enrolments_actor_consistent`).
  Enquiry flipped to CONVERTED, `converted_enrolment_id` linked
  correctly. Cleaned up the synthetic rows.
- **`/admin/enquiries`** — placeholder replaced with a real
  read-only cross-programme list. `requireAdminPermission(PERM_PROGRAMMES_VIEW)`
  gate (SUPER_ADMIN bypass). Service-role read embeds programme title
  + tutor name via `programme:nclex_programmes!inner(title,
  tutor:nclex_users!inner(name))` — single round trip. Client board
  has filter chips (status, identical set to tutor surface) plus a
  per-programme `<select>` dropdown that appears only when ≥2
  programmes have enquiries. Richer card layout: status pill +
  programme · tutor header line + lead identity + channel pills +
  message + "Open in tutor view ↗" link. **Status writes intentionally
  NOT on this surface** — every row's "Open in tutor view ↗" hops the
  admin to that programme's tutor queue, which has the actions (the
  same SUPER_ADMIN RLS bypass that lets the admin read on the admin
  surface lets them act on the tutor surface).

### What Slice 8 doesn't ship

- **No auto-forward email to the tutor** — no email infra yet. The
  tutor reads the lead on the queue and reaches out via the channel
  the student picked.
- **No "mark Converted" button.** Conversion is automatic via the 8c
  trigger when the enrolment lands by email match.
- **No CD-driven UI refinement yet** — Sam wants to bring a CD
  prototype to refine the tutor surface visual; the underlying data
  model + actions won't need to change for that.

### Open follow-on captured

- **Manual "mark converted" override** when the student enrols with a
  different email than they used in the enquiry (typo, work vs
  personal). Rare; deferred to v2.

## Session — 2026-05-23 (Payments 7e — retire `price_minor`)

The deliberate cutover that closes the payments arc. Slice 7a backfilled an
UPFRONT_FULL plan per programme so prices lived in two places (the legacy
`nclex_programmes.price_minor` column **and** the plan row), with
`syncUpfrontStrategy` mirroring writes between them — a stopgap so the public
surfaces could keep reading the simple column while installments/deposits/
schedule/sweep all switched to the plans table. 7e deletes the column.

### What shipped

- **Migration** `20260612120000_slice_7e_retire_programme_price_minor.sql`,
  applied to mynclex-dev:
  - DROP VIEW `nclex_public_programmes` (column removal requires DROP, not
    CREATE OR REPLACE).
  - Recreate the view **without** `p.price_minor` and **with** two derived
    columns: `headline_price_minor` = COALESCE(active UPFRONT_FULL's
    `total_price_minor` → MIN(initial) across other active plans → 0); and
    `headline_is_upfront` BOOLEAN so the UI knows whether the headline is a
    full price (case 1) or a first installment (case 2 — render "from ").
  - `ALTER TABLE nclex_programmes DROP COLUMN price_minor`.
  - Back-ported to `db/schema.sql` (column block) + `db/rls.sql` (view).
- **TS plumbing (8 files):**
  - `lib/programmes/types.ts` — drop `price_minor` from `Programme` /
    `ProgrammeListRow` / `ProgrammeFormValues`; add `upfront_total_minor`
    (nullable on the list row, required on the form) so the form has a
    pre-fill source on edit.
  - `lib/programmes/queries.ts` — `getMyProgrammes()` embeds the upfront
    plan via PostgREST `upfront:nclex_programme_payment_strategies(total_price_minor)`
    with `.eq('upfront.kind','UPFRONT_FULL')` (LEFT JOIN — every programme
    appears even if the upfront row is transiently missing).
  - `lib/programmes/actions.ts` — `CreateProgrammeInput` swaps `price_minor`
    for `upfront_total_minor`; create/edit no longer write `price_minor`;
    `syncUpfrontStrategy` goes from "mirror" to **authoritative writer** of
    full price. Same UPSERT shape; preserves `is_active` so a tutor who
    turned upfront off keeps it off when they later edit the price.
  - `lib/programmes/programme-form-modal.tsx` — Price field reads from
    `initial.upfront_total_minor` in edit mode; submit puts the value into
    `input.upfront_total_minor`.
  - `lib/programmes/edit-programme-trigger.tsx` — coalesces the nullable
    list-row `upfront_total_minor` to 0 when handing it to the modal
    (defensive — wouldn't normally be null since 7a backfilled everything).
  - `lib/discovery/types.ts` — `PublicProgramme` drops `price_minor`, gains
    `headline_price_minor` + `headline_is_upfront`.
  - `lib/strategies/types.ts` + `queries.ts` + `payment-plans-panel.tsx` —
    `PaymentPlansContext.programme.price_minor` removed; the panel derives
    its default-Total prop internally from `strategies.find(UPFRONT_FULL)?.total_price_minor`.
  - `lib/payments/init.ts` — PROGRAMME branch drops `price_minor` from the
    SELECT; when the caller sends no `strategyId` (back-compat), the
    fallback resolves the active UPFRONT_FULL plan and uses its
    `initial_price_minor` (a missing upfront → "That payment plan is not
    available").
- **UI surfaces (5 files):**
  - `app/(public)/programmes/programmes-list.tsx` — `priceParts` reads
    `headline_price_minor`; card renders "from " prefix when
    `!headline_is_upfront && headline_price_minor > 0`.
  - `app/(public)/programmes/[id]/page.tsx` — same prefix on the rail;
    `canEnrol` gate uses `headline_price_minor`; the sub-line splits into
    three cases (off-platform, 2+ plans, single upfront, single
    deposit/installments).
  - `app/(public)/checkout/programme/[id]/page.tsx` — gate + `programmeMinor`
    prop switched.
  - `app/(app)/tutor/programme/[programme_id]/payment-plans/page.tsx` —
    drops the `programmePriceMinor` prop (now derived inside the panel).
  - `styles/discovery.css` — `.from` style added to both card and rail.

### Verification

- `npx tsc --noEmit` — clean (vitest module-not-found is pre-existing
  worktree quirk — the `lib/payments/schedule.test.ts` file is unchanged
  and the binary is missing because worktrees skip `npm install` per the
  known lightningcss footgun).
- SQL sanity check on `nclex_public_programmes` — both seed programmes
  still resolve identical headlines to pre-cutover (₵3000 and ₵250),
  `headline_is_upfront = true` for both.
- Sam clicked through all 8 surfaces on localhost: discovery list, detail,
  checkout, tutor programmes list, edit pencil, new programme, payment-plans
  page, deactivate-upfront → from-prefix on card. All pass.

### Payments arc — complete

7e closes Phasing 2 and the multi-strategy payments arc that started with
slice 5.1 (basic ON_PLATFORM checkout) on 2026-05-18. The plans table is
now the single source of truth for amounts — no drift surface left.

## Session — 2026-05-22 (Payments 7d — installments lifecycle + reconciliation + grace + System Config)

Big payments session: shipped the whole recurring side of payment plans, then
two design-driven follow-ups from Sam's questions, then the first real admin
config surface. Three commits merged to `main` (1d160f9, 09b5180, 341e019);
System Config + a detail-page fix built + verified, pending commit at session end.

### 7d — installments lifecycle (commit 1d160f9)
- **Schedule engine** (`lib/payments/schedule.ts`) — one pure function:
  (frozen snapshot, enrolment anchor, payments-made) → next payment {index,
  amount, due} / fully-paid. 12 Vitest. Mirrored by the SQL sweep; the two
  must stay in lockstep. Added `vitest.config.ts` so tests resolve the `@/`
  alias (existing scoring tests dodged it with relative imports).
- **Installment payment path** — new `PROGRAMME_INSTALLMENT` checkout target;
  `init.ts` resolves the amount server-side from the snapshot (browser can't
  set it); `activate.ts` records it + auto-unpauses an `INSTALLMENT_OVERDUE`
  pause once caught up (never a `TUTOR_MANUAL` pause). New
  `/checkout/installment/[enrolmentId]` page reusing the checkout shell;
  reusable `<InstallmentCta>` on the student programme list.
- **Nightly sweep** (`20260608120000`) — `nclex_enrolment_nightly_sweep()`
  SECURITY DEFINER on pg_cron `0 2 * * *`: overdue→PAUSED, window→EXPIRED,
  lapsed ACTIVE subs→EXPIRED. Gated by `nclex_config.enrolment_sweep_enabled`
  (default ON; super-admin off-switch). **pg_cron enabled on dev** (1.6.4).
  The sweep does its own UPDATEs — the auth-gated `nclex_pause_enrolment` RPC
  can't run with no session + hardcodes `TUTOR_MANUAL` — so it's the single
  home for *system* transitions, as the RPCs are for *user* ones.
- **Read-time access gate** — `nclex_has_active_programme/cohort_enrolment`
  now also require `access_expires_at IS NULL OR > NOW()`, so a past-window
  enrolment loses access even before the sweep runs. Overdue is left to the
  sweep (not inlined on the hot RLS path) — a deliberate, doc-aligned choice.
- **7c bug caught + reverted:** the planned `installment_index = 1` on the
  initial row violates the `installment_index_scope` CHECK (only
  `PROGRAMME_INSTALLMENT` may carry it) — would've broken the INIT insert.
  Initial stays NULL (implicitly position 1); installments carry their position.
- Sam browser-tested: a real Paystack installment + a tutor mark-paid both
  recorded correctly and auto-unpaused.

### Follow-ups from Sam's questions (commit 09b5180)
- **Q1 — "how do we know a tutor-marked payment brought no money to QAcademy?"**
  Was inferred from a null `paystack_reference`. Now explicit:
  `collection_channel` (PAYSTACK / OFF_PLATFORM) + `recorded_by_user_id`
  (off-platform only) on `nclex_payments` (`20260609120000`); mark-paid stamps
  both. Backfilled by the reference heuristic.
- **Q2 — "mark-paid ≠ pause override; conflating them defeats on-platform
  payment."** Correct. Built **grace** (`20260610120000`):
  `installment_grace_until` (sweep-respected deadline) + `grace_history_json`
  (append-only audit). `giveMoreTimeAction` defers the pause WITHOUT recording
  a payment (installment still owed, on-platform, by the later date). Bare
  Resume kept (legit "let them in for now") but the sweep re-pauses overdue
  students that night — so Resume / Give more time / Mark paid each now carry
  a consequence-explaining overlay. Grace history stored as a column (not a
  table) per Sam — fine for the per-student story; promote later only if
  cross-student grace reporting is needed.

### Roster payment-column labels (commit 341e019)
Every roster row now reads meaningfully instead of a bare "—": Paid in full
(has plan, settled) / Off-platform (tutor-added) / Granted (admin) / Next… /
Overdue / Grace.

### System Config admin page (built, pending commit)
First real admin surface — replaced the `/admin/config` placeholder, built off
the back of the sweep flag. Typed editors over `nclex_config`: inline yes/no
switch for `enrolment_sweep_enabled` (confirm-on-off) + percent modal for
`bank_optin_discount`. Driven by a per-key definitions list (`config-defs.ts`:
label / description / type / validation) so a value can't be saved in the wrong
shape; `SYSTEM_MANAGE`-gated **service-role** write (the table's RLS is
SUPER_ADMIN-only for writes, but SYSTEM_MANAGE is the real gate for this
surface). Also fixed the programme **detail page**'s hardcoded "40% off" add-on
blurb → now reads the live discount (`getBankOptinDiscountPct()`, hides at 0%);
the checkout page was already dynamic. Surfaced when Sam set the discount to 20%
and the detail page still said 40%.

### Gotcha — lightningcss / CSS broke mid-session
Running `npm install` (to get vitest) tripped the npm optional-deps bug and
dropped Tailwind's `lightningcss` native binary, breaking the dev server's CSS.
Fix: copy `node_modules/lightningcss-win32-x64-msvc/*.node` into
`node_modules/lightningcss/`, clear `.next`, restart. May recur on any
`npm install`; clean fix is delete `node_modules` + `package-lock.json` and reinstall.

### Dev test data (left in place)
`TEST_7D`-tagged enrolments in the Bootcamp Q3 cohort + a self-paced one,
spanning every state (upcoming / overdue-paused / deposit / paid-in-full /
tutor-added off-platform), plus their seeded payment rows. The dev discount was
left at whatever Sam last set it to. Ask to clean up the `TEST_7D` rows when
done poking.

### Merge / release state
- Merged to `main`: `1d160f9` (7d), `09b5180` (follow-ups), `341e019` (roster labels).
- Built + verified, **pending commit**: System Config page + detail-page fix.
- **prod untouched** — three migrations (`20260608/09/10120000`) are dev-only;
  they apply to prod (and enable pg_cron there) only at the next `main → prod`
  release. Run the migration-tracker pre-check first.

### Next
- 7e (retire `price_minor`), or rotate per the alternate-features rule.
- Optional: clean up `TEST_7D` dev data; release `main → prod`.

## Session — 2026-05-22 (Payments Slice 5.6 — bank entitlement gating)

Made the bank subscription actually mean something: bank practice now
requires active bank access. Discussed the policy + every layer with Sam
before building.

### Policy settled with Sam

- **Full-lock on lapse** (Sam's call): once access expires you lose
  *everything* bank — builder, runner, AND history/review — like other
  platforms. Not just "can't start new attempts."
- **SUPER_ADMIN bypass** kept (tooling/testing); ensure a test student has a
  live sub before flipping on.
- **Trial** counts as access automatically once built (it writes an ACTIVE
  `nclex_subscriptions` row), so no special-casing now.
- **"Active access"** = ACTIVE row, bank-family pack (BANK_DURATION/TRIAL),
  `end_at` null-or-future — date-compared directly so it's correct even
  before the deferred expiry-sweep cron flips ACTIVE→EXPIRED.

### Build (layered per the layered-access rule)

- **DB (hard backstop), migration `20260605120000` (dev only):** SQL helper
  `nclex_has_active_bank_access(uuid)` (STABLE SECURITY DEFINER; REVOKEd
  from anon/authenticated — internal) + the gate inside the SECURITY
  DEFINER `nclex_create_attempt` RPC (re-emitted via `pg_get_functiondef`
  to avoid transcription drift, gate added after the auth/source checks;
  SUPER_ADMIN bypass). All attempts from that RPC are CUSTOM_BUILT, so the
  gate is unconditional there. Functions live in migrations only —
  `db/schema.sql` carries no function bodies, so nothing to mirror.
- **TS guard:** `lib/access/student/require-active-bank-subscription.ts`
  (barrel-exported) → `requireStudent` + SUPER_ADMIN bypass +
  `bankAccessForUser` (new `lib/payments/entitlements.ts`, also exposes
  `getMyBankAccess()`); redirect to `/bank-access` on no access.
- **Applied at the bank layout** (`app/(app)/student/bank/layout.tsx`) —
  one gate covers every `/student/bank/*` page (replaced the inline
  STUDENT-role check).
- **Source-aware runner gate** (`/session/[id]`, in the `(focused)` group,
  *outside* the bank layout — needs its own check): gates **only**
  `attempt.source === 'CUSTOM_BUILT'`. Tutor/programme quizzes are
  `PROGRAMME_ASSIGNED` (confirmed in the launch RPC) → skipped, so a
  bank-less programme student still runs their quizzes. (Sam flagged this
  exact risk mid-build; traced the data to confirm it's handled.)
- **Picker** (`/student/picker`): replaced the hard-coded
  `hasBankSubscription`/`bankDaysLeft` with real `getMyBankAccess()` —
  active → enters bank w/ "X days left" / "Lifetime access"; none/lapsed →
  "Get access →" routing to `/bank-access` (not the dashboard, which would
  only bounce a sub-less student).

### Verified

Helper returns correct per-student on dev: `+mynclexstudent` (30d) &
`+checkout` (365d) → true; `+mynclexstudent2` (no sub) → false; super-admin
→ false-by-helper but bypasses in guard/RPC. Routes compile (anonymous curl
307s, no 500s). tsc clean. **Full per-user behaviour (redirects, create-RPC
refusal, programme-quiz-still-works for a bank-less student) is Sam's
browser test** — committed but NOT merged to main until he's tested.

### Test matrix for Sam

- `+mynclexstudent` / `+checkout` (have sub) → bank works.
- `+mynclexstudent2` (enrolment, NO bank sub) → bounced from `/student/bank/*`
  to `/bank-access`; picker card shows "Get access →"; **programme quiz
  still runs** (the source-aware bit).
- `+mynclexsuperadmin` → in regardless (bypass).

### Files touched

- `db/migrations/20260605120000_slice_5_6_bank_entitlement_gate.sql` (new)
- `lib/payments/entitlements.ts` (new),
  `lib/access/student/require-active-bank-subscription.ts` (new),
  `lib/access/index.ts`
- `app/(app)/student/bank/layout.tsx`,
  `app/(app)/(focused)/session/[attempt_id]/page.tsx`,
  `app/(app)/student/picker/page.tsx`

### Resume next time

After Sam's browser test → merge 5.6 to main. Then Slice 5.7 — **my-payments
page** (student transaction list: date, product, amount, currency, status).
Then the deferred trail: trial self-serve signup, readiness purchase, the
pg_cron EXPIRED/PAUSED sweep, admin-grant enrolment. Migration
`20260605120000` is dev-only — ships to prod on the next release (now 3
pending: 5.4a cohort_id, 5.4b checkout_group, 5.6 bank gate).

## Session — 2026-05-22 (Payments Slice 5.5 — standalone bank landing + purchase)

Continued from 5.4b. Built the public standalone bank purchase, extracted
the shared checkout shell (the deferred 5.4b refactor), and designed a
first-cut `/bank-access` landing with Sam in the live preview.

### Design + scope settled with Sam

- **No CD prototype exists for a standalone bank page** (the handoff has
  only discovery/checkout/dashboard/tutor-workspace). Designed it ourselves;
  Sam adopted the first cut as a starting point.
- **Route named `/bank-access`** (Sam's call; renamed from the initial
  `/bank`). Checked for conflicts first — every existing `bank` folder is
  prefixed (`/student/bank`, `/tutor/bank`, `/admin/bank`), so a top-level
  `/bank-access` (or `/bank`) was clear. Nav had an inert "Practice bank"
  placeholder + a parked GHS/USD toggle — both now real.
- **Free trial deferred.** The landing shows it but the button is inert: a
  free 7-day trial needs a free **self-serve signup** path we don't have
  (students arrive by invite or pay-first today). Its own slice later.
- **Shell extraction now, not in 5.4b** — correct moment is the *second*
  consumer (bank), which shapes the abstraction. Did it as the opening move.

### Build

- **Shared checkout shell** `components/checkout/checkout-shell.tsx` (new
  folder, flagged to Sam): owns email + dup-check + logged-in prefill +
  own-email skip, the order rail (line items + total), Pay, what-happens-
  next. Products supply `lineItems`, `railHint`, `nextSteps`, `buildTarget()`
  and an optional `validate()`, plus their own panel as children. Exports a
  shared `money()` formatter.
- **Programme checkout** moved to `app/(public)/checkout/programme/[id]/`
  (`page.tsx` + `programme-checkout.tsx`) using the shell; old
  `checkout/[programmeId]/` deleted; Enrol link + (kept) callback updated.
- **Bank landing** `app/(public)/bank-access/` (`page.tsx` server fetches the
  5 paid BANK_DURATION tiers + credits from `nclex_products`;
  `bank-plans.tsx` client = GHS/USD toggle + tier cards + "Get access" →
  `/checkout/bank?product=&currency=`). `styles/bank.css`.
- **Bank checkout** `app/(public)/checkout/bank/` (`page.tsx` reads
  `?product=&currency=`, server-validates active paid BANK_DURATION else
  `redirect('/bank-access')`; `bank-checkout.tsx` = single bank line via the
  shell). No engine change — BANK init/verify/activate already shipped in
  5.2/5.3.
- Nav "Practice bank" → `/bank-access`; checkout.css gains `.co-plan-*` /
  `.co-change-link`.

### Verified (dev, end-to-end)

Freed the two prior test accounts first (`+checkout`, `+checkout2`) by
deleting from `auth.users` — cascades to profile/enrolments/subscriptions/
roles; `nclex_payments.user_id` is SET NULL so the financial audit rows
survive (confirmed: 4 payment rows kept, 0 still linked). Then pay-first
GHS BANK_365D as `+checkout`: payment INIT→PAID→ACTIVATED (₵700, single-item
group) → invite → `/welcome` → profile + STUDENT role → `nclex_subscriptions`
ACTIVE, `SELF_PURCHASE`, 365-day end. Programme checkout re-verified working
post-refactor; old `/checkout/[id]` 404s; invalid bank product redirects.
tsc clean. USD not fully testable (Paystack test account has USD disabled) —
same code path, different price column.

### Files touched

- `components/checkout/checkout-shell.tsx` (new)
- `app/(public)/bank-access/{page,bank-plans}.tsx` (new),
  `app/(public)/checkout/bank/{page,bank-checkout}.tsx` (new),
  `app/(public)/checkout/programme/[id]/{page,programme-checkout}.tsx` (new),
  removed `app/(public)/checkout/[programmeId]/*`
- `app/(public)/layout.tsx`, `app/(public)/programmes/[id]/page.tsx`,

Slice 5.6 — **bank entitlement gating**: bank/practice surfaces require an

guard, per the layered-access rule). Today bank/practice is gated only by
`require-student`; subscription entitlement is net-new. Then 5.7 (my-payments
page). **Trial signup** + the readiness purchase path remain deferred.

## Session — 2026-05-22 (Payments Slice 5.4b — bank opt-in at programme checkout)

Continued straight on from 5.4a (same working session, date rolled over).
Made the "Add bank access" card live: a student buys a programme **and** a
bank pack in **one Paystack charge**. Plus the logged-in-buyer prefill we'd
deferred. Two architecture questions settled with Sam before any code.

### Decisions settled with Sam (before code)

- **Combined-charge mechanics.** A combined order is *one charge, two
  things*. Kept one-purpose-per-row (clean ledger) and tied the rows with a
  shared `checkout_group_id` + a shared `paystack_reference`. That forced
  dropping the per-row UNIQUE on `paystack_reference` (a group legitimately
  shares it); uniqueness of a charge is guaranteed by our reference
  generation. Verify now loads the group by reference and checks Paystack's
  amount against the **sum** of the group's rows.
- **One payments table, not split per product type** (Sam asked). Kept the
  single `nclex_payments` ledger: payments are a ledger you always want in
  one list (my-payments / receipts / refunds / reconciliation), and the
  combined charge would be *harder* across two tables (the group would
  straddle them). Type-specific complexity (installments) lives in its own
  tables, not by splitting payments.
- `checkout_group_id` also future-proofs any later multi-product cart —
  Sam's point.

### Build (bottom-up)

- **Schema** (`20260604120000`, dev only): `checkout_group_id UUID NOT NULL
  DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()` (volatile default backfills every existing row
  with its own group); dropped `nclex_payments_paystack_reference_key`;
  added plain indexes on reference + group. Mirrored to `db/schema.sql`.
- **`init.ts`** rebuilt around a `LineItem[]` order. BANK target → one line;
  PROGRAMME target → programme line + optional **bank opt-in** line in the
  **programme's currency**, priced **server-side** as product ×
  (1 − `nclex_config.bank_optin_discount`), rounded. All lines share one
  generated reference + group id; Paystack is charged the **combined
  total**. Only paid `BANK_DURATION` tiers are opt-in-eligible.
- **`verify.ts`** group-aware: load all rows for the reference, FAILED-if-any,
  ALREADY-if-all-settled, else sum the amounts, verify once, **sum-matched**
  tamper-guard, flip the still-INIT rows PAID.
- **`activate.ts`** refactored to a **group orchestrator**: identity resolved
  once, the pay-first Supabase invite fires **once per group** (was per
  row), then each row grants (programme → enrolment, bank → subscription,
  `source=PROGRAMME_OPTIN` — that branch already existed from 5.3). Outcome
  precedence INVITE_SENT > PENDING_APPROVAL > ACTIVATED > ALREADY.
  `activatePendingForEmail` now groups by `checkout_group_id`. `VerifyResult`
  trimmed (no per-row purpose — a group has several).
- **UI**: live opt-in card (checkbox **off by default**, 5 tiers w/
  discounted + struck prices, **90d default once ticked**, empty-state when
  off); order summary gains the bank line + combined total; **logged-in
  prefill** (own email pre-filled + skips the dup-block). Detail-rail
  bank-hint flipped from "coming soon" to live. New `.co-bank-*` CSS.

### Verified (dev, end-to-end)

Pay-first guest (`+checkout2`), GHS: programme ₵3,000 + 365-day tier ₵420 =
**₵3,420 single charge**. Both rows shared one group + reference, both
ACTIVATED. Enrolment `PENDING_APPROVAL` (365-day window); bank subscription
`ACTIVE` immediately, `PROGRAMME_OPTIN`, `BANK_365D`. One invite for the
group → `/welcome` → both activated. tsc clean.

### Deferred

- **Shared checkout-shell extraction + route rename**
  (`/checkout/programme/[id]`) → **Slice 5.5**. Extracting a reusable shell
  from one consumer is premature; the shape becomes clear when standalone
  bank (5.5) is the second concrete case. Recommended this to Sam mid-build;
  he agreed ("then we move to 5.5").

### Files touched

- `db/migrations/20260604120000_slice_5_4b_checkout_group.sql` (new),
  `db/schema.sql`
- `lib/payments/{init,verify,activate,types}.ts`
- `app/(public)/checkout/[programmeId]/{page,checkout-form}.tsx`,
  `app/(public)/programmes/[id]/page.tsx`
- `styles/checkout.css`

### Resume next time

Slice 5.5 — standalone bank landing (public, GHS|USD dual-currency toggle, 5
tiers, pay-first), opening with the **shared checkout-shell extraction +
route rename** (the deferred 5.4b refactor, now with two consumers to shape
it). Then 5.6 (bank entitlement gating), 5.7 (my-payments page). Migration
`20260604120000` is **dev only** — ships to prod on the next release.

## Session — 2026-05-21 (Payments Slice 5.4a — programme-only on-platform checkout)

Made the **Enrol** button real. Built the programme checkout end-to-end
(upfront-full only), extended the activation engine to programme
enrolment, and rebuilt the detail-page rail to the CD prototype. Verified
the whole loop on dev. Continues the 5.x arc (5.1 schema, 5.2 Paystack
init/verify, 5.3 bank activation — already in BUILD_LIST).

### Scope decision (before any code)

The CD prototype's checkout has one **Pay** button covering programme +
bank opt-in, but our `nclex_payments` is one-purpose-per-row with a unique
reference — so a single Paystack charge can't cleanly span a programme AND
a bank purchase. Three options weighed (programme-only first / two
sequential charges / one charge + `checkout_group_id`). **Chose to split
the slice:** ship **5.4a** programme-only now (smallest testable loop, no
schema-stretch), and do the bank opt-in as **5.4b** using the **one-charge
`checkout_group_id`** model — two sequential Paystack redirects rejected as
bad UX for a phone-first audience. Sam added: render the strategy +
bank-opt-in cards as **placeholders** now so the page reads as the full
prototype.

### Build

- **DB.** New nullable `nclex_payments.cohort_id` (FK → `nclex_cohorts`,
  RESTRICT; CHECK: only on `PROGRAMME_INITIAL`). The enrolment row can't
  exist until *after* payment (an abandoned checkout would otherwise leave
  an orphan `PENDING_APPROVAL` row tripping the unique-active guard), so
  the payment is the only thing that can carry the picked cohort across the
  Paystack round trip. Migration `20260603120000` (dev only) + mirrored
  into `db/schema.sql`.
- **Activation** (`activate.ts`). Added `grantProgrammeEnrolment`:
  `PROGRAMME_INITIAL` → an `nclex_enrolments` row — tutor-led
  `PENDING_APPROVAL`, self-paced `ENROLLED` — `SELF_PAID`,
  `enrolled_by_user_id` NULL, `access_expires_at` frozen from
  `access_window_days`. Idempotent: an existing active enrolment is *linked*
  to the payment, never re-created (don't trap a paid buyer behind the
  unique-active index); 23505-on-insert treated as success. Same two
  identity cases as bank (existing account → grant now; pay-first guest →
  invite → `/welcome` → `activatePendingForEmail`). New
  outcome `PENDING_APPROVAL` threaded through `ActivateOutcome` →
  `SettleResult` → callback copy.
- **`init.ts`.** Programme branch now enforces published + on-platform and
  validates the chosen cohort belongs to the programme and is joinable
  (cancelled_at IS NULL + upcoming-or-late-join); writes `cohort_id`.
  (Caught a bug mid-build: `nclex_cohorts` has no `status` column —
  cancellation is `cancelled_at`.)
- **Checkout page.** New public route
  `app/(public)/checkout/[programmeId]/` — server component re-applies the
  Enrol enable gate (hand-typed URL can't reach an un-enrollable
  programme); co-located client form does cohort pick (collapses for one),
  email + live dup-check (existing account → "log in to continue"), order
  summary, **Pay with Paystack** (calls `startPaymentAction`, navigates to
  the returned URL). Strategy + bank-opt-in cards are disabled "coming
  soon" placeholders. New `styles/checkout.css` (`.co-*`, public bridge
  tokens). Callback page gains the `PENDING_APPROVAL` message.
- **Detail page.** Live **Enrol** `Link` → `/checkout/[id]` when
  on-platform + public price + something joinable; else the disabled
  "coming soon" button. **Rail rebuilt to the CD prototype**: price +
  sub-line, Enrol + cohort note, bank-opt-in hint box, payment-strategies
  list (Upfront live; Deposit/Installments as muted placeholders).
  Seat counts deliberately omitted (public view has none — Slice 3c).
  `.pub-btn-primary` made flex-centred so it renders right as an `<a>`.

### UX call surfaced mid-session

Wiring the Enrol button, I'd gated the **waitlist** off when checkout went
live (Enrol + waitlist felt like two competing primaries). Sam flagged the
disappeared button; discussed (pay-now vs talk-first are different
intents; a waitlist lead on an on-platform programme also quietly
undercuts on-platform collection). **Reverted for now** — both show — with
the proper treatment (likely a smaller "prefer to talk first?" secondary
link, option 3) deferred to a later decision.

### Verified (dev, end-to-end)

Flipped the bootcamp to GHS ₵3,000 on-platform tutor-led. Pay-first guest
(`mybackpacc+checkout@gmail.com`) → payment `INIT→PAID→ACTIVATED`
(amount-matched, `cohort_id` set, `enrolment_id` backfilled) → enrolment
`PENDING_APPROVAL` / `SELF_PAID` / 365-day window → `/welcome` created
profile + STUDENT role → tutor (`+mynclextutor`) approved →
`ENROLLED`, `access_expires_at` unchanged on approval. tsc clean.

### Deferred

- **5.4b** — live bank opt-in card (`checkout_group_id` one-charge model).
- **Logged-in-buyer prefill** — today a logged-in buyer who types their own
  email hits the dup-block; checkout is built for the prospect path.
  Session-aware prefill is a clean follow-up (folded into 5.4b).

### Op note

This worktree spawned **without** its `.env.local` (the `.worktreeinclude`
auto-copy didn't fire — a lone miss; 35/35 other post-mechanism worktrees
have it). Copied from the main checkout, restarted dev on :3000. If a fresh
worktree 404s every page with a Supabase-client error, that's the cause:
`cp ../../../.env.local .env.local`.

### Files touched

- `db/migrations/20260603120000_slice_5_4a_payment_cohort.sql` (new),
  `db/schema.sql`
- `lib/payments/{activate,init,settle,types}.ts`
- `app/(public)/checkout/[programmeId]/{page,checkout-form}.tsx` (new),
  `app/(public)/checkout/callback/page.tsx`,
  `app/(public)/programmes/[id]/page.tsx`, `app/(public)/layout.tsx`
- `styles/checkout.css` (new), `styles/discovery.css`

### Resume next time

5.4b (bank opt-in via `checkout_group_id` + logged-in prefill), or rotate
per the alternate-features rule (Bank 4.7 mark-for-review, or another
programme feature). Migration `20260603120000` is **dev only** — ships to
prod on the next release.

## Session — 2026-05-20 (Payments Slice 4 — student-initiated waitlist + cohort-workspace redesign)

Built **Slice 4** — the off-platform student-initiated waitlist —
end-to-end, then reworked the tutor surface to the CD design after Sam
flagged the first cut looked plain. Six sub-slices + a follow-up confirm.

### Build

- **4a — DB foundation.** New `nclex_cohort_waitlist` (PENDING /
  CONVERTED / DISMISSED leads; CASCADE off cohort+programme since these
  are pre-enrolment leads, not financial records — contrast
  `nclex_enrolments` RESTRICT). Anon-callable SECURITY DEFINER
  `nclex_join_waitlist` validates the cohort is genuinely joinable (same
  "open" definition as `nclex_public_programmes`) before inserting, and
  is idempotent per `(cohort, lower(email))` while PENDING (returns the
  existing row, no enumeration signal). Tutor-owns-programme SELECT RLS;
  convert/dismiss run service-side (no tutor write policy). Migration
  `20260531120000` (dev only); tracker version aligned to the filename.
- **4b — Tutor convert/dismiss.** Extracted a shared
  `inviteOrAttachAndEnrol` helper from `addStudentAction` (invite-or-
  attach + ENROLLED row, returns the new enrolment id); `convertWaitlist
  EntryAction` reuses it then marks the lead CONVERTED + links the
  enrolment; `dismissWaitlistEntryAction` marks DISMISSED. Ownership
  gated via RLS-scoped read, writes under service role.
- **4c — Public join form.** A **second** rail button on the programme
  detail page, *under* the (kept) "coming soon" online-enrolment CTA —
  Sam's call to reserve that button for Slice 5 checkout. Opens a form
  with a cohort picker that collapses to fixed text for a single open
  cohort. `joinWaitlistAction` → RPC; success state in the modal. Only
  shown for tutor-led programmes with ≥1 joinable cohort.

### Design rework (the substantive turn)

Sam pointed to the in-repo CD handoff
`docs/product-plan/design-handoff/payments-and-enrolment/prototypes/tutor-cohort-workspace.html`
(I'd been looking in Downloads — the payments handoff lives in the repo).
Agreed scope: adopt the prototype's **structure** but render with the
app's own tokens (not the mock's warm-indigo palette) for consistency.
The prototype is the full payments-era workspace, so payment-dependent
bits (Access·payment column, collection mode, Approve-modal payment
trail, seats) are **placeholders / deferred to Slices 5–7**.

- **4d — Contact capture (Sam's addition).** Added `phone` (optional) +
  `preferred_contact TEXT[]` (CALL / SMS / WHATSAPP / EMAIL, ≥1, default
  EMAIL) to the table + RPC. **Phone required iff a phone-based channel
  is chosen** — enforced at four layers: public form (native `required`),
  join action, RPC, and a table CHECK. WhatsApp-first ordering (dominant
  in Ghana).
- **4e — Workspace redesign.** Students page rebuilt: **Roster | Waitlist
  sub-tabs** (amber count), **summary cells** (Enrolled / Pending /
  Waitlist / Paused), **avatars** (initials) + **relative time**, roster
  as a table with status **filter chips** + **search** + the
  Access·payment placeholder column. Waitlist tab faithful to the
  prototype (avatar, contact line w/ phone + preferred-channel badges,
  message / "No message", Convert → / Dismiss). New
  `lib/enrolments/format.ts` (`initials`, `relativeTime`); dead
  `.enrol-page/head/roster/row` CSS removed, new `.cw-*` block added.
- **4f — Public form contact fields.** Phone + preferred-contact
  checkboxes wired through to the action + RPC.
- **Convert confirm (follow-up).** Sam asked whether Convert warned
  before firing — it didn't. Added a confirmation dialog spelling out the
  consequence (invite to `<email>` + Enrolled), matching our
  destructive/irreversible-action convention. Dismiss already had one.

### Discussion captured (not built)

- **Email validation.** We check *format* only (browser `type=email` +
  action regex + RPC), three layers — not deliverability. Decided not to
  add MX/3rd-party/round-trip verification at the interest stage: the
  Convert step's Supabase invite **is** the free real-world deliverability
  test, exactly when it matters, and the captured phone/WhatsApp is the
  fallback. A client-side "did you mean gmail.com?" typo hint is the only
  cheap upgrade on the table; deferred unless asked.

Verified: `tsc` clean throughout; both pages serve; RPC smoke-tested
(positive, idempotent dedup, bad email, fake/closed cohort, empty name,
WhatsApp-without-phone all behave). 3 dev leads seeded for browser
testing. Sam tested + approved.

## Session — 2026-05-20 (Payments Slice 3.5 — tutor public profile, JSONB)

Built **Slice 3.5** — the tutor public profile — design-discussed
heavily before any code (Sam-driven). Merged to `main`.

### Design discussion (the substantive part)

Two naming/scope decisions came out of pushback from Sam, both improving
on the plan in BUILD_LIST:

1. **Column named `public_profile`, not `profile`.** Sam flagged the
   column lives on the shared `nclex_users` table (students/admins too),
   so it isn't tutor-specific. Key realisation surfaced: with JSONB the
   only hard-to-reverse decision is the *column name* (rename = migration);
   the field shape is a TS type that reshapes for free. So we invested the
   decision in a good name. `public_` encodes the invariant — public-display
   data only; private/sensitive fields (DOB, contacts) go in dedicated
   columns. Distinguished the *profile page* (a user's private control
   panel, future) from the `public_profile` *column* (the outward slice);
   they nest, the prefix keeps them from blurring.

2. **Dropped the `display_mode` person/business switch.** Sam's call: for
   transparency, show person **and** business *together* when a business
   name is present — never one instead of the other. Kept **both** bios
   (`bio` + `business_bio`): a solo tutor's business bio matters now, and
   the person bio future-proofs co-tutor individual bios on a programme.

Field labels reworked for the tutor form (raw keys are unclear when
filling): headline → "Current role / title", speciality → "Speciality /
field", years_experience → "Years tutoring". Aligned to the
`discovery-and-detail.html` design handoff (card "By X · N years
tutoring"; header role sub-line; "About {name}" section).

### Build (4 sub-slices)

- **A** `public_profile JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'` on `nclex_users`
  (migration `20260530120000`) + `PublicProfile` type in
  `lib/discovery/types.ts` as the single source of truth.
- **B** Added `tutor_profile` (the whole bag) to `nclex_public_programmes`
  (migration `20260530130000`). Note: `CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW` only
  appends columns at the tail — hit `42P16` inserting mid-list, moved it
  to the end (readers use `SELECT *`).
- **C** `/tutor/profile` placeholder → real editor
  (`public-profile-form.tsx` + `actions.ts` + `styles/profile.css`):
  person + business sections, live preview, dirty-gated save, success/
  error toasts, **beforeunload** (hard exit) **+ in-app discard-overlay**
  (click-capture interceptor on in-app links, reuses `DiscardConfirm`).
  Added `.auth-toast-success` variant to `authoring.css`.
- **D** Shared `tutorAttribution` + `yearsTutoringLabel` in
  `lib/discovery/format.ts`; card + detail render attribution ("business
  · with person") + "About {person}" / "About {business}" sections.
  Avatar `<img>` fallback to initials. Seeded the dev tutor (4ed777d7).

Logo upload deferred (URL field for now, per Sam). `nclex_tutors` table
for private tutor data (vetting / $29-mo sub / payouts) still arrives
with the vetting work. Verified tsc + browser. Next: Slice 4 (waitlist).

## Session — 2026-05-20 (Payments Slice 3 — price deltas + public discovery + detail)

Built the whole of Payments **Slice 3** — programme price deltas, the
public discovery list, and the read-only detail page — across three
sub-slices, each described-before-built per Sam's request. Established
the **shared public-projection layer** (a key architectural decision).
Merged to `main`.

### 3a — DB price deltas + tutor form (migration `20260527120000`)

Dropped the dual `price_minor_ghs`/`price_minor_usd` columns for a single
tutor-chosen `price_currency` + `price_minor`; added
`payment_collection_mode` (OFF/ON_PLATFORM) + `access_window_days`
(nullable = lifetime). Backfill: non-zero column wins, GHS tiebreak (both
dev rows GHS). Reworked the tutor programme form (`lib/programmes/`):
currency picker + single price, plus a new "Access & collection" section.

**Currency discussion (Sam-initiated).** Sam questioned dropping dual
currency. Resolved by separating two things both called "dual currency":
(A) two independently-authored prices (PPP) — kept for the *bank*
(QAcademy-owned, deliberate GHS≠USD), dropped for *programmes* (one tutor,
one settlement currency); (B) showing the price converted to the viewer's
currency (FX display) — a presentation layer, deferred polish. Single
currency for programmes is correct; the FX "≈ equivalent" hint comes later.

### Two design clarifications (logged in payments-and-enrolment.md)

From a tutor-form review with Sam: (1) **self-paced no longer forced
on-platform** — downgraded from hard rule to smart default (off-platform
self-paced works via manual tutor-add); (2) **collection mode reframed** —
not two exclusive methods; manual tutor-add is *always* available, the
flag only governs whether the public page shows an online Paystack button.
Form relabelled "Online checkout: On/Off". No schema change.

### 3b — Shared public-projection layer + discovery (migration `20260528120000`)

The architectural crux. Instead of per-page read functions that would
re-derive "what's public," built **one shared public window**: the
`nclex_public_programmes` **view** (owner-rights, granted to anon) exposing
a curated projection — programme public fields + tutor **name/avatar only**
(never email/phone/role) + a cohort rollup (next open cohort +
open-cohort count). The "published + active tutor" gate lives once, in the
view. This **superseded and removed the 3a base-table public-read policy +
its `nclex_user_is_active` helper** — avoiding two mechanisms doing the
same job (Sam's explicit "don't repeat" steer).

New `(public)` route group: `app/(public)/layout.tsx` owns shared chrome —
public nav (full CD link set; Programmes + Log in live, the rest + GHS/USD
toggle as inert placeholders per Sam) + footer (`components/public/`).
Discovery list at `/programmes` (old placeholder deleted) — hero + working
client-side filter chips (All / Tutor-led / Self-paced) + card grid.
`lib/discovery/` (queries/types/format) + `styles/discovery.css` (token
bridge: handoff vocabulary → our tokens). Bank add-on hint line omitted
(advertises a checkout that doesn't exist yet).

**Filter bug during testing** was a stale dev-server route conflict (old
`app/programmes` + new `(public)/programmes` briefly co-existed → Turbopack
"two parallel pages" error broke client hydration → only the default "All"
worked). Disk was clean; restarting the dev server fixed it. Logic was
fine.

### 3c — Read-only detail page (migration `20260529120000`)

`/programmes/[id]` — reads the programme from the view by id (→ 404 if not
publicly visible). Added `nclex_public_units` (published units only) +
`nclex_public_cohorts` (non-cancelled) to the same view family. Renders:
header (tutor avatar/name/delivery tag + meta strip Length/Format/Access
window/Collection), About (programme description), Syllabus/Modules
(units), Available cohorts (tutor-led only; status pills, **no seat
counts** — deferred), pricing rail (headline price or "Contact for price"
+ **disabled "coming soon" Enrol** — checkout is Slice 5). Self-paced shows
"Modules", no cohorts section, "Start now" disabled.

### Tutor-profile decision (deferred to next session, by Sam's call)

The detail page's "About {tutor}" section needs tutor background, which has
no schema home. Sam pushed for a flexible **JSONB `profile`** on
`nclex_users` (role-agnostic, extensible) over fixed headline/bio columns.
Agreed, with refinements: a documented typed shape in TS as the single
source of truth, treat `profile` as public-display-only by rule. Corrected
that "multiple tutors per programme" is a co-tutor join-table concern, not
a profile-storage one. Captured as **Slice 3.5 (NEXT SESSION)** in
BUILD_LIST — not rushed. The detail page's tutor-About section is omitted
until then.

### Verified

`tsc` clean (only the 2 pre-existing vitest errors); `npm run build` clean.
Anon RLS verified for all three views (published + active-tutor gating,
inactive-tutor hides, units/cohorts curated). Browser-tested by Sam: 3a
form, 3b list + filters, 3c detail (both delivery modes) — PASS. Dev
migration trackers reconciled to filenames (`20260527/28/29120000`).
Merged to `main`.

## Session — 2026-05-20 (Enrolment — access-gating step + picker inline list)

Closed the access-gating step that Slice 2b deferred: enrolment status
went from *displayed* (pills) to *enforced*. Then, at Sam's direction,
rebuilt the picker to list enrolled programmes inline. Two commits,
FF-merged to `main` (`38cebc1 → 3ab2219`); prod untouched.

### Design decision — two visibility tiers

The crux: if RLS hid *everything* for a non-enrolled student, a
paused/cancelled student clicking their programme would hit a blank 404
— we couldn't even show "Bootcamp — Paused". So visibility splits:

- **Metadata tier** (programmes + cohorts) — readable with ANY enrolment
  row (active or terminal). Powers the switcher's status tiles; also
  drops the old `status = 'PUBLISHED'` gate entirely so an enrolled
  student keeps access after the tutor ARCHIVES the programme (per the
  access-window rule). Public discovery of unenrolled programmes is
  Slice 3, a separate surface.
- **Content tier** (units / blocks / activities / cohort checklist /
  tutor_quizzes / programme_quizzes) — readable only with an ENROLLED
  row. The real gate.

Only `ENROLLED` opens content; PENDING/PAUSED/REJECTED/CANCELLED/EXPIRED
all block it (matches payments-and-enrolment.md lifecycle table).

### DB — migration `20260526120000_enrolments_access_gating.sql`

Four SECURITY DEFINER helpers (`nclex_has_programme_enrolment`,
`nclex_has_active_programme_enrolment`, + cohort variants) mirroring the
`nclex_user_has_role` pattern (SECURITY DEFINER so a policy referencing
`nclex_enrolments` can't recurse through its own RLS; `auth.uid()` still
reads the caller's JWT). The 8 `*_student_select` policies rewritten
into the two tiers. Mirrored into `db/rls.sql`. **Launch RPCs:** both
`nclex_create_programme_attempt` + `nclex_create_standalone_quiz_attempt`
are SECURITY DEFINER (bypass RLS), so policy tightening doesn't reach
them — added an active-programme-enrolment guard (SUPER_ADMIN bypass for
QA preview); the programme-attempt RPC now resolves programme_id via the
activity's unit to run the check. Applied to dev via MCP; tracker
version reconciled `20260520121257 → 20260526120000` to match the
filename (the drift CLAUDE.md warns about).

### TS layer

- `requireStudentProgrammeAccess` / `requireStudentCohortAccess` now
  resolve enrolment status (new `getMyProgrammeEnrolmentStatus` /
  `getMyCohortEnrolmentStatus` in `lib/enrolments/queries.ts`,
  active-wins-over-terminal). No enrolment row → `notFound()` (metadata
  RLS already returns null); has a row but not ENROLLED → `redirect`
  to `/student/picker`. This is the **safety net** for direct-URL /
  bookmarked access — the primary UX is the switcher hiding entry.
- Switcher (`programme-switcher-overlay`): non-ENROLLED rows render
  non-clickable + dimmed with the status pill + a reason line. The
  query auto-narrows to enrolled-only via the tightened metadata RLS
  (no query-body change, just a doc-comment update).

### Picker rebuilt (Sam-directed; not in the handoff)

The handoff has no picker screen — Sam's call: the picker should *list*
enrolled programmes inline rather than open the popup. Extracted the
popup's row rendering into a shared `<ProgrammeList>`
(`components/nav/student/programme-list.tsx`) used by both surfaces, so
they can't drift. Picker now has stacked sections — "Your programmes"
(inline list, or a disabled "Browse programmes — coming soon" empty
card) + "Question Bank". The popup is now purely for in-programme
switching. Removed the old hard-coded `hasProgrammeEnrolment`
placeholder. Bonus: a bounced paused student lands on the picker and
immediately sees their programme + reason.

### Verified

- RLS impersonation (`SET request.jwt.claims`) for both seeded students:
  - welcometest2 (ENROLLED in one cohort + PAUSED in another of the same
    programme): 1 programme, both cohorts visible (metadata), content +
    enrolled-cohort checklist visible, **PAUSED cohort checklist = 0**.
  - mynclexstudent2 (CANCELLED only): programme + cohort visible
    (metadata, for the pill), **all content = 0**.
  - The unenrolled "Self-Paced Refresher" correctly vanished from both.
- `npx tsc` clean (only the 2 pre-existing vitest errors); `npm run
  build` clean (×2). Security advisors: the new helpers carry the same
  benign `*_security_definer_function_executable` WARN as the existing
  `nclex_user_has_role` family (anon → `auth.uid()` NULL → false); no
  new errors.
- No self-paced enrolments in dev, so that path is logic-mirrored not
  browser-tested. Sam tested the gating + picker in-browser: PASS.

### Design-tooling note

Confirmed with Sam: the enrolment handoff
(`docs/product-plan/design-handoff/payments-and-enrolment/`) is the
**baseline we build to, not a rigid spec** — modify/add when warranted,
flagging deviations for sign-off (the picker being the first). Its 4
prototypes: Cohort workspace / Dashboard programme tile states /
Discovery list + programme detail / On-platform checkout + /welcome.

### Next

- **Slice 3** (programme price/access-window deltas + public discovery
